CASE refs_whitespace
  S r [a=1&2] @2:16
  C len=2 "\n " @3:2
  S x [] @3:5
  C len=1 "1" @3:7
  C len=2 ".5" @3:12
  E x @3:16
  S y [] @3:20
  E y @3:20
  E r @3:24
  OK
CASE qualified_normalised
  S a:r [x=1& 2 3] @2:4
  S b [] @2:8
  E b @2:8
  C len=1 "t" @2:10
  C len=1 "A" @2:14
  E a:r @2:20
  OK
CASE cdata_comment
  S a:r [x=1\n2 3 4] @3:4
  S b [] @3:7
  C len=1 "x" @3:9
  C len=2 "&y" @3:15
  C len=1 "A" @3:19
  E b @3:23
  S c [] @3:27
  E c @3:27
  C len=1 "t" @3:38
  C len=3 "<z>" @3:53
  E a:r @3:59
  OK
CASE text_comment_text
  S r [] @1:4
  C len=1 "a" @1:5
  C len=1 "b" @1:14
  E r @1:18
  OK
CASE text_pi_text
  S r [] @1:4
  C len=1 "a" @1:5
  C len=1 "b" @1:14
  E r @1:18
  OK
CASE text_cdata_text
  S r [] @1:4
  C len=1 "a" @1:5
  C len=1 "b" @1:18
  C len=1 "c" @1:19
  E r @1:23
  OK
CASE cdata_empty
  S r [] @1:4
  C len=1 "a" @1:5
  C len=0 "" @1:17
  C len=1 "c" @1:18
  E r @1:22
  OK
CASE predefined_refs_only
  S r [] @1:4
  C len=1 "<" @1:9
  C len=1 ">" @1:13
  C len=1 "&" @1:18
  C len=1 """ @1:24
  C len=1 "'" @1:29
  E r @1:33
  OK
CASE char_refs_mixed
  S r [] @1:4
  C len=1 "x" @1:6
  C len=2 "Ay" @1:13
  C len=2 "Bz" @1:18
  E r @1:22
  OK
CASE crlf_text
  S r [] @1:4
  C len=5 "a\nb\nc" @3:2
  E r @3:6
  OK
CASE attr_ws
  S r [x= a b c d , y=\t\n\r] @3:25
  E r @3:25
  OK
CASE big_text
  S r [] @1:4
  C len=100000 "aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa...<100000 units>" @1:100004
  E r @1:100008
  OK

CASE mismatched_end_line2
  S r [] @1:4
  C len=1 "\n" @2:1
  S x [] @2:4
  FATAL line=2 col=6 expected end of tag 'x'
  THROWN SAXParseException
CASE error_line3
  S r [] @1:4
  C len=1 "\n" @2:1
  S x [] @2:4
  C len=1 "\n" @3:1
  FATAL line=3 col=3 expected end of tag 'x'
  THROWN SAXParseException
CASE two_roots
  S r [] @1:5
  E r @1:5
  FATAL line=1 col=5 comment or processing instruction expected
  THROWN SAXParseException
CASE text_before_root
  FATAL line=1 col=1 invalid document structure
  THROWN SAXParseException
CASE undeclared_entity

  FATAL line=1 col=9 entity 'foo' not found
  THROWN SAXParseException
CASE duplicate_attr

  THROWN SAXParseException
CASE doctype_internal

  C len=2 "EE" @1:38
  E r @1:42
  OK
CASE latin1_declared

  C len=1 "é" @1:48
  E r @1:52
  OK

  FATAL line=1 col=8 invalid character reference
  THROWN SAXParseException

  THROWN SAXParseException

  FATAL line=1 col=7 hex radix character references must use 'x', not 'X'
  THROWN SAXParseException

  THROWN SAXParseException
CASE unclosed

  FATAL line=1 col=4 input ended before all started tags were ended; last tag started is 'r'
